# Agents · C · part 12

869 entries, client-writer to cluster-ops. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **client-writer**: Implements the client side of a planned feature under client/ with components, contexts, hooks, lib and pages. Does not write tests. Run after server-writer so the real API is known. ([JonatanOrdonez/fullstack-harness](https://github.com/JonatanOrdonez/fullstack-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/client-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cliente**: Representante do usuário no Escritório. Entrevista o usuário até entender o problema, responde dúvidas do Atendente em nome dele e faz a auditoria final de todo o escopo com o perfil de critérios calibrado pelo feedback do usuário. Usado apenas pelo fluxo /escritorio:demanda. ([Henriquetin/escritorio-agentes](https://github.com/Henriquetin/escritorio-agentes/blob/HEAD/plugins/escritorio/agents/cliente.md))
- **cliente-drawer-integrar**: Implementador especializado da integração legacy WR Comercial/Delphi → drawer Cliente 760px (ADR 0179). Domina os 5 tabs (Identificação/Contato/Endereço/Comercial/Classificação + Auditoria), os 7 endpoints PATCH em `Modules/Crm/Http/Controllers/ClienteAutosaveController`, o payload `ContactController::buildClienteIndexCustomers`, e o mapping canon dos ~14 cols Bucket A + ~7-10 cols Bucket B (ADR 0195/0197) que JÁ EXISTEM em `contacts` via migrations 2026_05_27_120000 + 2026_05_27_140000 mas ainda NÃO TÊM UI nem API PATCH cobrindo. Ativar quando Wagner pedir: - "implementar campo X no drawer"… ([wagnerra23/oimpresso.com](https://github.com/wagnerra23/oimpresso.com/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cliente-drawer-integrar.md))
- **cliente-simulado**: Hace de varios clientes de un negocio y platica con el bot REAL de IaRadio (chat web, agente con herramientas, citas, pedidos, catálogo y base de conocimiento, con los modelos de IA de verdad) sobre una base local desechable, y reporta dónde el bot inventa, se traba o no hace lo que el cliente pidió. Úsalo después de cambiar el agente del cliente, los prompts del bot, citas, pedidos o catálogo, y antes de pedirle al usuario su prueba real. No toca producción ni manda WhatsApp. ([josealfredo79/adRadio](https://github.com/josealfredo79/adRadio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cliente-simulado.md))

- **clientgen-reader**: Read-only fetcher for the ClientGen connector. Given a question (which templates fit, who is on a list, what is on the calendar, what did a past order say), it calls only the ClientGen read tools and returns a compact, structured answer. Never drafts, approves, cancels, or logs anything. Dispatched by the ClientGen skills when they need facts before drafting. ([clientgen/clientgen-reach](https://github.com/clientgen/clientgen-reach/blob/HEAD/agents/clientgen-reader.md))
- **clientrouter-lifecycle-auditor**: Use this agent on any page or component with a client &lt;script&gt; before it ships, and on any bug report of the form 'works on first load, dead after navigating'. It audits the script's lifecycle under Astro's ClientRouter — init on astro:page-load, no module-scope DOM capture, document/window listeners replaced not stacked, config re-read per load — and reports file:line findings in a fixed table. The mechanical half (DOMContentLoaded-only init) is already a ratchet (tests/clientrouter-init-ratchet.test.ts); this agent does the judgment half the scanner cannot. It never edits. Examples: &lt;exampl… ([braven112/mfl.football.v2](https://github.com/braven112/mfl.football.v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/clientrouter-lifecycle-auditor.md))
- **clients**: Android Use is a local stdio MCP server: command `au`, arguments `serve --mcp`. It starts even when no device is connected; tool results then explain what to fix. ([austinintelligence/android-use](https://github.com/austinintelligence/android-use/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/clients.md) · ★36)
- **clients**: Knossos is an MCP server first, so any client that speaks MCP over stdio can use its tools. This page registers it with Codex and with a client that reads the common `mcpServers` shape. The pane, the notes and the session brief are Claude Code hooks and run only there; for Claude Code, see [installation](../get-started/installation.md) and [the plugin](../claude-code/plugin.md). ([AraneaDev/knossos](https://github.com/AraneaDev/knossos/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/clients.md) · ★8)
- **CLIENTS**: `AGENTS.md` and the top-level `rules/*.md` files are the canonical rulebook. Through `init-rulebook`, the index loads always-on rules eagerly, task-conditional rules for matching work, and action-gated rules immediately before matching operations; client projections are delivery mechanisms only and do not own membership or applicability. ([t4sh/dotfiles](https://github.com/t4sh/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/CLIENTS.md) · ★2)
- **clients**: Every MCP client connects to Cloudhal in one of two ways ([which one?](/docs/agents#two-ways-to-connect)): ([cloudhal-dev/cloudhal](https://github.com/cloudhal-dev/cloudhal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/clients.md) · ★1)
- **clif-buddy-tester**: Use when buddy testing, validating, or QA-ing a CLIF study kit before it is distributed to the consortium — verifying it runs at your site, produces every promised poolable artifact, and is PHI-safe. Also use to audit a buddy test report, or to investigate why a CLIF pipeline's output looks wrong or implausible. Reports evidence-backed findings; leaves blocking/PI/clinical-plausibility calls to the human. Ships with the clif-icu plugin. ([Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills](https://github.com/Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/clif-buddy-tester.md) · ★3)
- **clif-code-reviewer**: Review CLIF code in R, Python, SQL, or notebooks for case/snake_case bugs, schema and vocabulary mismatches, and macOS/Linux/Windows portability. Confirm the user's CLIF version and current mCIDE before checking filters, joins, recodes, and scores. ([Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills](https://github.com/Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/clif-code-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **clif-phi-auditor**: Use before sharing, pushing, or distributing ANY CLIF artifact — a repo, results tables, figures, logs, or a study-kit output bundle — to scan it for PHI leakage and small-cell risk. Also use when asked "is this safe to share?". Reports evidence-backed findings with file:line cites; the human makes the final share/no-share call. ([Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills](https://github.com/Common-Longitudinal-ICU-data-Format/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/clif-phi-auditor.md) · ★3)

- **clin-coder-cdi**: Use when a clinical episode's documentation is too thin, ambiguous, or contradictory to code confidently, and a clarification is needed from the treating clinician. Drafts a neutral, non-leading Clinical Documentation Improvement (CDI) query identifying the gap and what would resolve it. Read-only; it never codes and never puts words in the clinician's mouth. Pairs with the clin-coder skill. ([devdaviddr/ai-resources](https://github.com/devdaviddr/ai-resources/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/clin-coder-cdi.md))
- **clin-coder-concept-extractor**: Use when extracting codeable clinical concepts (diagnoses, procedures, findings) with exact evidence spans AND clinical context (negation, temporality, certainty, family history, laterality) from a single clinical document — e.g. one discharge summary, operation note, or pathology report. Returns structured concepts only; it does not assign codes. Ideal to run one per document, in parallel, for the clin-coder skill. ([devdaviddr/ai-resources](https://github.com/devdaviddr/ai-resources/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/clin-coder-concept-extractor.md))
- **clin-coder-verifier**: Use to adversarially audit a proposed clinical coding before a coder sees it. Runs the clin-coder engine's validity edits, confirms every code exists, is evidence-grounded, and cites an applicable coding-standard rule, and checks sequencing / condition-onset / withholding. Read-only — it cannot modify the proposal. Pairs with the clin-coder skill. ([devdaviddr/ai-resources](https://github.com/devdaviddr/ai-resources/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/clin-coder-verifier.md))
- **cline**: Cline CLI 3.0.70 and the VS Code extension 4.1.23 can send OpenAI-compatible chat completions to Rapid-MLX. - **Clinical Evidence Agent**: Evidence standards and clinical credibility framework for AI agents operating in healthcare contexts. Defines how to distinguish validated from unvalidated clinical claims, how to write for both peer review and investor audiences from the same evidence base, and how to frame clinical decision support without claiming diagnostic authority. - **cloud-functions-specialist.client-guards.knowledge**: - **Client burst guards are `rateLimitStamped(type, s, key)`** (ADR-0020): same-request stamp on `request.time` + `lastDocId`. - **cloud-sessions**: - The shell is Linux with GNU userland. - GitHub's GraphQL API is closed to cloud sessions.
